QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version\n// \n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5","id":"msg_011CehSkCtXu9SzinHEF7FV3","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01AX78FWhK3BLPMeqMZkZNhp","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/qtclustering_claude-sonnet-5-cc-medium_cuda_r2/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp","content":"// QT Clustering Benchmark - CUDA Parallel Version\n//\n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n//\n// The GPU parallelization works as follows:\n//  - The full pairwise point-distance matrix is computed once on the GPU.\n//  - For every \"round\" of the sequential QT outer loop, one CUDA thread\n//    block per still-unclustered candidate seed grows a candidate cluster\n//    in parallel (all seeds are tried simultaneously). Within a block, the\n//    N points are distributed across threads; a running \"distance to\n//    cluster\" value per point is maintained incrementally and a block-wide\n//    argmin reduction (with the same tie-break rule as the reference\n//    implementation) selects the next point to add to that seed's cluster.\n//  - The winning seed (largest cluster, first occurrence wins on ties,\n//    exactly like the sequential reference) is picked on the host from the\n//    per-seed cardinalities, and its member list is copied back from the\n//    device.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                       \\\n    do {                                                                       \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                            \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                            \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__,   \\\n                    __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err__));                      \\\n            exit(1);                                                          \\\n        }                                                                      \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points (host-side, used for validation)\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA kernels\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Compute the full N x N pairwise Euclidean distance matrix.\n__global__ void computeDistanceMatrixKernel(const double* __restrict__ xs,\n                                             const double* __restrict__ ys,\n                                             float* __restrict__ dist,\n                                             int N) {\n    int i = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    int j = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i < N && j < N) {\n        double dx = xs[i] - xs[j];\n        double dy = ys[i] - ys[j];\n        dist[static_cast<size_t>(i) * N + j] = static_cast<float>(sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy));\n    }\n}\n\n// One block per candidate seed. Grows a candidate cluster from that seed and\n// records the resulting member list and cardinality.\n//\n// value[i] semantics:\n//   - INF  -> point i is unavailable (already globally clustered, or already\n//             a member of the cluster being grown in this block)\n//   - else -> current maximum distance from point i to all cluster members\n//             added so far (0 before any distance has been folded in)\n__global__ void growClustersKernel(const float* __restrict__ dist,\n                                    const bool* __restrict__ clusteredGlobal,\n                                    const int* __restrict__ seedIndices,\n                                    int numSeeds,\n                                    float threshold,\n                                    int N,\n                                    float* __restrict__ valueBuf,   // numSeeds * N\n                                    int* __restrict__ membersBuf,   // numSeeds * N\n                                    int* __restrict__ cardinalityOut) {\n    const int b = blockIdx.x;\n    if (b >= numSeeds) return;\n\n    const int seed = seedIndices[b];\n    float* value = valueBuf + static_cast<size_t>(b) * N;\n    int* members = membersBuf + static_cast<size_t>(b) * N;\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int nthreads = blockDim.x;\n    const float kInf = std::numeric_limits<float>::infinity();\n\n    __shared__ int s_count;\n    __shared__ int s_newMember;\n    __shared__ bool s_done;\n\n    extern __shared__ unsigned char s_mem[];\n    float* s_bestVal = reinterpret_cast<float*>(s_mem);\n    int* s_bestIdx = reinterpret_cast<int*>(s_bestVal + nthreads);\n\n    // Initialize value[] based on global clustered state.\n    for (int i = tid; i < N; i += nthreads) {\n        value[i] = clusteredGlobal[i] ? kInf : 0.0f;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    if (tid == 0) {\n        value[seed] = kInf;\n        members[0] = seed;\n        s_count = 1;\n        s_newMember = seed;\n        s_done = false;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    while (s_count < N) {\n        const int newMember = s_newMember;\n        const float* distRow = dist + static_cast<size_t>(newMember) * N;\n\n        // Fold the newly added member's distances into the running max.\n        for (int i = tid; i < N; i += nthreads) {\n            if (value[i] != kInf) {\n                value[i] = fmaxf(value[i], distRow[i]);\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        // Find the point with the smallest running max-distance that is\n        // still below the threshold (argmin, smallest index wins ties).\n        float bestVal = kInf;\n        int bestIdx = -1;\n        for (int i = tid; i < N; i += nthreads) {\n            const float v = value[i];\n            if (v < threshold && v < bestVal) {\n                bestVal = v;\n                bestIdx = i;\n            }\n        }\n        s_bestVal[tid] = bestVal;\n        s_bestIdx[tid] = bestIdx;\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        for (int stride = nthreads / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {\n            if (tid < stride) {\n                const float otherVal = s_bestVal[tid + stride];\n                const int otherIdx = s_bestIdx[tid + stride];\n                const float myVal = s_bestVal[tid];\n                const int myIdx = s_bestIdx[tid];\n                bool takeOther = (otherIdx >= 0) &&\n                                  (myIdx < 0 || otherVal < myVal ||\n                                   (otherVal == myVal && otherIdx < myIdx));\n                if (takeOther) {\n                    s_bestVal[tid] = otherVal;\n                    s_bestIdx[tid] = otherIdx;\n                }\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n        }\n\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            const int chosen = s_bestIdx[0];\n            if (chosen < 0) {\n                s_done = true;\n            } else {\n                value[chosen] = kInf;\n                members[s_count] = chosen;\n                s_count++;\n                s_newMember = chosen;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (s_done) break;\n    }\n\n    if (tid == 0) {\n        cardinalityOut[b] = s_count;\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Host-side driver\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (GPU-parallel across candidate seeds)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                   const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    // --- Upload point coordinates and compute the full distance matrix ---\n    std::vector<double> xs(N), ys(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        xs[i] = points[i].x;\n        ys[i] = points[i].y;\n    }\n\n    double *d_xs = nullptr, *d_ys = nullptr;\n    float* d_dist = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_xs, sizeof(double) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_ys, sizeof(double) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dist, sizeof(float) * static_cast<size_t>(N) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_xs, xs.data(), sizeof(double) * N, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_ys, ys.data(), sizeof(double) * N, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    {\n        dim3 block(16, 16);\n        dim3 grid((N + block.x - 1) / block.x, (N + block.y - 1) / block.y);\n        computeDistanceMatrixKernel<<<grid, block>>>(d_xs, d_ys, d_dist, N);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_xs));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_ys));\n\n    // --- Persistent device buffers reused across outer-loop rounds ---\n    bool* d_clustered = nullptr;\n    int* d_seedIndices = nullptr;\n    float* d_valueBuf = nullptr;\n    int* d_membersBuf = nullptr;\n    int* d_cardinality = nullptr;\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered, sizeof(bool) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_seedIndices, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_valueBuf, sizeof(float) * static_cast<size_t>(N) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_membersBuf, sizeof(int) * static_cast<size_t>(N) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinality, sizeof(int) * N));\n\n    std::vector<int> hostSeedIndices(N);\n    std::vector<int> hostCardinality(N);\n    std::vector<int> hostClustered(N); // used only for host<->bool conversion staging\n\n    const int threadsPerBlock = (N < 256) ? std::max(32, N) : 256;\n    const size_t sharedMemBytes =\n        static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) * (sizeof(float) + sizeof(int));\n\n    // Stage the (initially all-false) clustered array once.\n    {\n        std::vector<unsigned char> initClustered(N, 0);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_clustered, initClustered.data(), sizeof(bool) * N,\n                               cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    }\n\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const int numSeeds = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n        for (int i = 0; i < numSeeds; ++i) {\n            hostSeedIndices[i] = unclustered_indices[i];\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_seedIndices, hostSeedIndices.data(),\n                               sizeof(int) * numSeeds, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n        growClustersKernel<<<numSeeds, threadsPerBlock, sharedMemBytes>>>(\n            d_dist, d_clustered, d_seedIndices, numSeeds,\n            static_cast<float>(threshold), N, d_valueBuf, d_membersBuf, d_cardinality);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostCardinality.data(), d_cardinality,\n                               sizeof(int) * numSeeds, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n        // First occurrence of the maximum cardinality wins (matches the\n        // sequential reference's strict \"cardinality > max_cardinality\").\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_block = -1;\n        for (int i = 0; i < numSeeds; ++i) {\n            if (hostCardinality[i] > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = hostCardinality[i];\n                best_block = i;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (best_block >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            std::vector<int> best_cluster_members(max_cardinality);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(best_cluster_members.data(),\n                                   d_membersBuf + static_cast<size_t>(best_block) * N,\n                                   sizeof(int) * max_cardinality, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = unclustered_indices[best_block];\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n\n            for (int m : best_cluster_members) {\n                clustered[m] = true;\n            }\n\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                                [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end());\n\n            for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n                hostClustered[i] = clustered[i] ? 1 : 0;\n            }\n            std::vector<unsigned char> packed(N);\n            for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) packed[i] = static_cast<unsigned char>(hostClustered[i]);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_clustered, packed.data(), sizeof(bool) * N,\n                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n        } else {\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_dist));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_clustered));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_seedIndices));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_valueBuf));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_membersBuf));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cardinality));\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                       const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                       const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                              points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; Within a block, the\n//    N points are distributed across threads; a running \"distance to\n//    cluster\" value per point is maintained incrementally and a block-wide\n//    argmin reduction (with the same tie-break rule as the reference\n//    implementation) selects the next point to add to that seed's cluster.\n//  - The winning seed (largest cluster, first occurrence wins on ties,\n//    exactly like the sequential reference) is picked on the host from the\n//    per-seed cardinalities, and its member list is copied back from the\n//    device.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                       \\\n    do {                                                                       \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                            \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                            \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__,   \\\n                    __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err__));                      \\\n            exit(1);                                                          \\\n        }                                                                      \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points (host-side, used for validation)\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA kernels\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Compute the full N x N pairwise Euclidean distance matrix.\n__global__ void computeDistanceMatrixKernel(const double* __restrict__ xs,\n                                             const double* __restrict__ ys,\n                                             float* __restrict__ dist,\n                                             int N) {\n    int i = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    int j = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i < N && j < N) {\n        double dx = xs[i] - xs[j];\n        double dy = ys[i] - ys[j];\n        dist[static_cast<size_t>(i) * N + j] = static_cast<float>(sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy));\n    }\n}\n\n// One block per candidate seed. Grows a candidate cluster from that seed and\n// records the resulting member list and cardinality.\n//\n// value[i] semantics:\n//   - INF  -> point i is unavailable (already globally clustered, or already\n//             a member of the cluster being grown in this block)\n//   - else -> current maximum distance from point i to all cluster members\n//             added so far (0 before any distance has been folded in)\n__global__ void growClustersKernel(const float* __restrict__ dist,\n                                    const bool* __restrict__ clusteredGlobal,\n                                    const int* __restrict__ seedIndices,\n                                    int numSeeds,\n                                    float threshold,\n                                    int N,\n                                    float* __restrict__ valueBuf,   // numSeeds * N\n                                    int* __restrict__ membersBuf,   // numSeeds * N\n                                    int* __restrict__ cardinalityOut) {\n    const int b = blockIdx.x;\n    if (b >= numSeeds) return;\n\n    const int seed = seedIndices[b];\n    float* value = valueBuf + static_cast<size_t>(b) * N;\n    int* members = membersBuf + static_cast<size_t>(b) * N;\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int nthreads = blockDim.x;\n    const float kInf = std::numeric_limits<float>::infinity();\n\n    __shared__ int s_count;\n    __shared__ int s_newMember;\n    __shared__ bool s_done;\n\n    extern __shared__ unsigned char s_mem[];\n    float* s_bestVal = reinterpret_cast<float*>(s_mem);\n    int* s_bestIdx = reinterpret_cast<int*>(s_bestVal + nthreads);\n\n    // Initialize value[] based on global clustered state.\n    for (int i = tid; i < N; i += nthreads) {\n        value[i] = clusteredGlobal[i] ? kInf : 0.0f;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    if (tid == 0) {\n        value[seed] = kInf;\n        members[0] = seed;\n        s_count = 1;\n        s_newMember = seed;\n        s_done = false;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    while (s_count < N) {\n        const int newMember = s_newMember;\n        const float* distRow = dist + static_cast<size_t>(newMember) * N;\n\n        // Fold the newly added member's distances into the running max.\n        for (int i = tid; i < N; i += nthreads) {\n            if (value[i] != kInf) {\n                value[i] = fmaxf(value[i], distRow[i]);\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        // Find the point with the smallest running max-distance that is\n        // still below the threshold (argmin, smallest index wins ties).\n        float bestVal = kInf;\n        int bestIdx = -1;\n        for (int i = tid; i < N; i += nthreads) {\n            const float v = value[i];\n            if (v < threshold && v < bestVal) {\n                bestVal = v;\n                bestIdx = i;\n            }\n        }\n        s_bestVal[tid] = bestVal;\n        s_bestIdx[tid] = bestIdx;\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        for (int stride = nthreads / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {\n            if (tid < stride) {\n                const float otherVal = s_bestVal[tid + stride];\n                const int otherIdx = s_bestIdx[tid + stride];\n                const float myVal = s_bestVal[tid];\n                const int myIdx = s_bestIdx[tid];\n                bool takeOther = (otherIdx >= 0) &&\n                                  (myIdx < 0 || otherVal < myVal ||\n                                   (otherVal == myVal && otherIdx < myIdx));\n                if (takeOther) {\n                    s_bestVal[tid] = otherVal;\n                    s_bestIdx[tid] = otherIdx;\n                }\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n        }\n\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            const int chosen = s_bestIdx[0];\n            if (chosen < 0) {\n                s_done = true;\n            } else {\n                value[chosen] = kInf;\n                members[s_count] = chosen;\n                s_count++;\n                s_newMember = chosen;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (s_done) break;\n    }\n\n    if (tid == 0) {\n        cardinalityOut[b] = s_count;\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Host-side driver\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (GPU-parallel across candidate seeds)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                   const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    // --- Upload point coordinates and compute the full distance matrix ---\n    std::vector<double> xs(N), ys(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        xs[i] = points[i].x;\n        ys[i] = points[i].y;\n    }\n\n    double *d_xs = nullptr, *d_ys = nullptr;\n    float* d_dist = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_xs, sizeof(double) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_ys, sizeof(double) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dist, sizeof(float) * static_cast<size_t>(N) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_xs, xs.data(), sizeof(double) * N, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_ys, ys.data(), sizeof(double) * N, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    {\n        dim3 block(16, 16);\n        dim3 grid((N + block.x - 1) / block.x, (N + block.y - 1) / block.y);\n        computeDistanceMatrixKernel<<<grid, block>>>(d_xs, d_ys, d_dist, N);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_xs));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_ys));\n\n    // --- Persistent device buffers reused across outer-loop rounds ---\n    bool* d_clustered = nullptr;\n    int* d_seedIndices = nullptr;\n    float* d_valueBuf = nullptr;\n    int* d_membersBuf = nullptr;\n    int* d_cardinality = nullptr;\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered, sizeof(bool) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_seedIndices, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_valueBuf, sizeof(float) * static_cast<size_t>(N) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_membersBuf, sizeof(int) * static_cast<size_t>(N) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinality, sizeof(int) * N));\n\n    std::vector<int> hostSeedIndices(N);\n    std::vector<int> hostCardinality(N);\n    std::vector<int> hostClustered(N); // used only for host<->bool conversion staging\n\n    const int threadsPerBlock = (N < 256) ? std::max(32, N) : 256;\n    const size_t sharedMemBytes =\n        static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) * (sizeof(float) + sizeof(int));\n\n    // Stage the (initially all-false) clustered array once.\n    {\n        std::vector<unsigned char> initClustered(N, 0);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_clustered, initClustered.data(), sizeof(bool) * N,\n                               cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    }\n\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const int numSeeds = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n        for (int i = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                              points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; 1;","         }","     }","-    ","+","     return 0;"," }"]}],"originalFile":"// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version\n// \n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n               num_points, threshold);\n        return 1;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n    printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Generate synthetic data\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n    \n    // Perform QT clustering\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);\n    \n    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n        cluster_end - cluster_start);\n    \n    printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n    printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n    \n    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics\n    int total_clustered = 0;\n    int max_cluster_size = 0;\n    \n    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {\n        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());\n        total_clustered += size;\n        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n    }\n    \n    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : \n        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n    \n    printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\", \n           total_clustered, num_points, \n           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);\n    printf(\"Average cluster size: %.2f\\n\", avg_cluster_size);\n    printf(\"Maximum cluster size: %d\\n\", max_cluster_size);\n    \n    // Performance metrics\n    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;\n    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;\n    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\\n\", \n           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing\n        std::vector<double> membershipData;\n        membershipData.reserve(num_points);\n        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);\n            }\n        }\n        for (int m : membership) {\n            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));\n        }\n        print_results(membershipData, \"ClusterMembership\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n Within a block, the\n//    N points are distributed across threads; a running \"distance to\n//    cluster\" value per point is maintained incrementally and a block-wide\n//    argmin reduction (with the same tie-break rule as the reference\n//    implementation) selects the next point to add to that seed's cluster.\n//  - The winning seed (largest cluster, first occurrence wins on ties,\n//    exactly like the sequential reference) is picked on the host from the\n//    per-seed cardinalities, and its member list is copied back from the\n//    device.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include                                            \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__,   \\\n                    __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err__));                      \\\n            exit(1);                                                          \\\n        }                                                                      \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points (host-side, used for validation)\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA kernels\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Compute the full N x N pairwise Euclidean distance matrix.\n__global__ void computeDistanceMatrixKernel(const double* __restrict__ xs,\n                                             const double* __restrict__ ys,\n                                             float* __restrict__ dist,\n                                             int N) {\n    int i = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    int j = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i < N && j < N) {\n        double dx = xs[i] - xs[j];\n        double dy = ys[i] - ys[j];\n        dist[static_cast<size_t>(i) * N + j] = static_cast<float>(sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy));\n    }\n}\n\n// One block per candidate seed. Grows a candidate cluster from that seed and\n// records the resulting member list and cardinality.\n//\n// value[i] semantics:\n//   - INF  -> point i is unavailable (already globally clustered, or already\n//             a member of the cluster being grown in this block)\n//   - else -> current maximum distance from point i to all cluster members\n//             added so far (0 before any distance has been folded in)\n__global__ void growClustersKernel(const float* __restrict__ dist,\n                                    const bool* __restrict__ clusteredGlobal,\n                                    const int* __restrict__ seedIndices,\n                                    int numSeeds,\n                                    float threshold,\n                                    int N,\n                                    float* __restrict__ valueBuf,   // numSeeds * N\n                                    int* __restrict__ membersBuf,   // numSeeds * N\n                                    int* __restrict__ cardinalityOut) {\n    const int b = blockIdx.x;\n    if (b >= numSeeds) return;\n\n    const int seed = seedIndices[b];\n    float* value = valueBuf + static_cast<size_t>(b) * N;\n    int* members = membersBuf + static_cast<size_t>(b) * N;\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int nthreads = blockDim.x;\n    const float kInf = std::numeric_limits<float>::infinity();\n\n    __shared__ int s_count;\n    __shared__ int s_newMember;\n    __shared__ bool s_done;\n\n    extern __shared__ unsigned char s_mem[];\n    float* s_bestVal = reinterpret_cast<float*>(s_mem);\n    int* s_bestIdx = reinterpret_cast<int*>(s_bestVal + nthreads);\n\n    // Initialize value[] based on global clustered state.\n    for (int i = tid; i < N; i += nthreads) {\n        value[i] = clusteredGlobal[i] ? kInf : 0.0f;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    if (tid == 0) {\n        value[seed] = kInf;\n        members[0] = seed;\n        s_count = 1;\n        s_newMember = seed;\n        s_done = false;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    while (s_count < N) {\n        const int newMember = s_newMember;\n        const float* distRow = dist + static_cast<size_t>(newMember) * N;\n\n        // Fold the newly added member's distances into the running max.\n        for (int i = tid; i < N; i += nthreads) {\n            if (value[i] != kInf) {\n                value[i] = fmaxf(value[i], distRow[i]);\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        // Find the point with the smallest running max-distance that is\n        // still below the threshold (argmin, smallest index wins ties).\n        float bestVal = kInf;\n        int bestIdx = -1;\n        for (int i = tid; i < N; i += nthreads) {\n            const float v = value[i];\n            if (v < threshold && v < bestVal) {\n                bestVal = v;\n                bestIdx = i;\n            }\n        }\n        s_bestVal[tid] = bestVal;\n        s_bestIdx[tid] = bestIdx;\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        for (int stride = nthreads / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {\n            if (tid < stride) {\n                const float otherVal = s_bestVal[tid + stride];\n                const int otherIdx = s_bestIdx[tid + stride];\n                const float myVal = s_bestVal[tid];\n                const int myIdx = s_bestIdx[tid];\n                bool takeOther = (otherIdx >= 0) &&\n                                  (myIdx < 0 || otherVal < myVal ||\n                                   (otherVal == myVal && otherIdx < myIdx));\n                if (takeOther) {\n                    s_bestVal[tid] = otherVal;\n                    s_bestIdx[tid] = otherIdx;\n                }\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n        }\n\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            const int chosen = s_bestIdx[0];\n            if (chosen < 0) {\n                s_done = true;\n            } else {\n                value[chosen] = kInf;\n                members[s_count] = chosen;\n                s_count++;\n                s_newMember = chosen;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (s_done) break;\n    }\n\n    if (tid == 0) {\n        cardinalityOut[b] = s_count;\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Host-side driver\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (GPU-parallel across candidate seeds)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                   const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    // --- Upload point coordinates and compute the full distance matrix ---\n    std::vector<double> xs(N), ys(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        xs[i] = points[i].x;\n        ys[i] = points[i].y;\n    }\n\n    double *d_xs = nullptr, *d_ys = nullptr;\n    float* d_dist = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_xs, sizeof(double) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_ys, sizeof(double) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dist, sizeof(float) * static_cast<size_t>(N) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_xs, xs.data(), sizeof(double) * N, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_ys, ys.data(), sizeof(double) * N, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    {\n        dim3 block(16, 16);\n        dim3 grid((N + block.x - 1) / block.x, (N + block.y - 1) / block.y);\n        computeDistanceMatrixKernel<<<grid, block>>>(d_xs, d_ys, d_dist, N);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_xs));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_ys));\n\n    // --- Persistent device buffers reused across outer-loop rounds ---\n    bool* d_clustered = nullptr;\n    int* d_seedIndices = nullptr;\n    float* d_valueBuf = nullptr;\n    int* d_membersBuf = nullptr;\n    int* d_cardinality = nullptr;\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered, sizeof(bool) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_seedIndices, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_valueBuf, sizeof(float) * static_cast<size_t>(N) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_membersBuf, sizeof(int) * static_cast<size_t>(N) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinality, sizeof(int) * N));\n\n    std::vector<int> hostSeedIndices(N);\n    std::vector<int> hostCardinality(N);\n    std::vector<int> hostClustered(N); // used only for host<->bool conversion staging\n\n    const int threadsPerBlock = (N < 256) ? std::max(32, N) : 256;\n    const size_t sharedMemBytes =\n        static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) * (sizeof(float) + sizeof(int));\n\n    // Stage the (initially all-false) clustered array once.\n    {\n        std::vector<unsigned char> initClustered(N, 0);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_clustered, initClustered.data(), sizeof(bool) * N,\n                               cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    }\n\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const int numSeeds = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n        for (int i = 0; i < numSeeds; ++i) {\n            hostSeedIndices[i] = unclustered_indices[i];\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_seedIndices, hostSeedIndices.data(),\n                               sizeof(int) * numSeeds, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n        growClustersKernel<<<numSeeds, threadsPerBlock, sharedMemBytes>>>(\n            d_dist, d_clustered, d_seedIndices, numSeeds,\n            static_cast<float>(threshold), N, d_valueBuf, d_membersBuf, d_cardinality);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostCardinality.data(), d_cardinality,\n                               sizeof(int) * numSeeds, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n        // First occurrence of the maximum cardinality wins (matches the\n        // sequential reference's strict \"cardinality > max_cardinality\").\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_block = -1;\n        for (int i = 0; i < numSeeds; ++i) {\n            if (hostCardinality[i] > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = hostCardinality[i];\n                best_block = i;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (best_block >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            std::vector<int> best_cluster_members(max_cardinality);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(best_cluster_members.data(),\n                                   d_membersBuf + static_cast<size_t>(best_block) * N,\n                                   sizeof(int) * max_cardinality, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = unclustered_indices[best_block];\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n\n            for (int m : best_cluster_members) {\n                clustered[m] = true;\n            }\n\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                                [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end());\n\n            for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n                hostClustered[i] = clustered[i] ? 1 : 0;\n            }\n            std::vector<unsigned char> packed(N);\n            for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) packed[i] = static_cast<unsigned char>(hostClustered[i]);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_clustered, packed.data(), sizeof(bool) * N,\n                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n        } else {\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_dist));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_clustered));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_seedIndices));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_valueBuf));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_membersBuf));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cardinality));\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                       const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                       const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                              points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; };\nstruct Cluster { std::vector<int> members; int seed_point; };\n\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    while (count < N) {\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) group_cnt = N - count;\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) continue;\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    return closest_point;\n}\n\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, threshold, point_count);\n        if (closest < 0) break;\n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    if (cluster_members) *cluster_members = members;\n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, threshold, N, &candidate_members);\n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end());\n        } else break;\n    }\n    return clusters;\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool printResults = true;\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n    }\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);\n    printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n    if (printResults) {\n        std::vector<double> membershipData;\n        membershipData.reserve(num_points);\n        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c)\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i)\n                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        for (int m : membership) membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));\n        print_results(membershipData, \"ClusterMembership\");\n    }\n    return 0;\n}\nEOF\ncp -r /home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/qtclustering_claude-sonnet-5-cc-medium_cuda_r2/common /tmp/\ng++ -O2 -std=c++20 /tmp/orig/qtclustering.cpp -o /tmp/orig/qtclustering_ref\n/tmp/orig/qtclustering_ref -n 1000 -t 2.0","uuid":"96c7ed6b-9c14-43be-afb9-ab560425ba13","session_id":"4c37483b-c100-4a5a-b2e2-eee80cc469ee"}
